- Timestamp correction (2026-10-10): the preceding interpretation was wrong
  because the read-only investigation connection used MySQL's `SYSTEM` timezone
  (KSA), while the application connection explicitly sets its session to UTC.
  For position `35`, MySQL's KSA session renders `opened_at` as
  `2026-10-09 23:45:06.682175`; converting that instant to UTC yields
  `2026-10-09 20:45:06.682175`. The Inspector's main trade card parses backend
  timestamps as UTC and renders them in the browser-local timezone. Therefore,
  with a KSA browser, its displayed 23:45 entry time is correct. Do not add
  three hours to the value shown by this KSA-session SQL connection.
- Signal confirmation: `trade_signal.id=89469` was generated at
  `2026-10-09 23:45:03.498678` KSA (`20:45:03.498678` UTC), from the 1m
  candle opened at 23:44 KSA. It is `WATCH`, score 60, confidence 74,
  `WEAK_UPTREND`, `TREND_FOLLOWING`, and `final_entry_allowed=1`.
- Entry: `0.000005410`; stop `0.000005382950`; TP `0.000005433730`; the
  25%-size setup-wakeup route entered from a 5m BUY with 1h NEUTRAL context.
- Exit: highest recorded price before close was `0.000005430`, or 84.2815% of
  the original TP distance. The dynamic lock activated at `0.000005421865` and
  live price `0.000005420` breached it. At the breach the current 1m and 5m
  were WATCH and 1h was NEUTRAL. The shared exit policy therefore returned
  `PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT`; it requires bullish 1h plus supportive 5m, or WATCH 1h
  plus supportive 5m, to hold a breached lock.
- TP extension: not evaluated. Extension begins only once price is at or above
  the original TP. The recorded high remained below TP at the time of exit.
- Subsequent action: later local candles reached `0.000005500` at about 00:40
  KSA, above the original TP. This is hindsight, not evidence available at the
  exit. Local SHIB 1m/5m coverage ends at about 00:56 KSA, so any claimed
  continuation through 12:15 KSA is not verifiable from local candle evidence.
- Assessment: behavior followed the existing protection rule, without a
  confirmed execution or latency defect. A future exit-policy change could
  explicitly allow a near-TP WATCH/WATCH/NEUTRAL continuation hold under
  measurable momentum/structure conditions and a tighter safety floor; it
  requires a separate design and Replay validation.

# SHIBUSDT #89469 near-TP continuation investigation Г?" 2026-10-10

- Status: investigation approved; implementation is explicitly not approved.
  This workstream must not alter or delay FIX-153 release work.
- Hypothesis to test: a breached profit lock close to target may sometimes
  continue when current market structure and momentum remain supportive.
- Important counter-hypothesis: `1h=NEUTRAL` is not positive continuation
  evidence merely because 1m/5m are `WATCH`. Relaxing that guard on distance
  to TP alone could create false holds in weak or sideways markets.
- Required evidence before a policy change: use historical Replay/production
  evidence to compare the current exit rule against a hypothetical bounded
  hold using only information available at the breach. Segment results by
  1h/5m context, regime, trend structure, momentum, retracement depth and
  target-progress band. Evaluate TP hit rate, retained profit, adverse move,
  drawdown, and false-hold frequency. No post-exit price may inform entry or
  exit eligibility.
- Rejected approach: do not authorize continuation from `WATCH/WATCH/NEUTRAL`
  or near-TP distance alone.
- Code-review refinement: `PositionExitPolicy` creates a sharp tier boundary:
  with supportive 5m/current evidence, 1h `WATCH` can hold a breached lock but
  1h `NEUTRAL` falls through to `PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT`. Existing FIX-11T near-TP
  protection arms at 90% target progress and is evaluated after the ordinary
  lock-breach exit, so it could not affect #89469 at 84.28% progress.
- Fee-model refinement: `WalletAutoExecutionService` writes `fee_usdt=0` for
  paper BUY, SELL, and near-TP partial-harvest executions. Any policy Replay
  comparison must add an explicit fee/slippage model before claiming protected
  gross profit is a net gain.
- Approved experiment protocol from independent review: Production policy is
  unchanged. Compare (A) existing immediate lock exit, (B) a one-window,
  near-TP continuation using existing time-safe structure/momentum/context
  calculations, and (C) a volatility-adjusted tighter trailing exit. Use a
  pre-registered 85% TP-progress primary threshold; #89469 at 84.28% does not
  qualify and must not drive threshold selection. Require non-bearish 5m/1h,
  a fee-adjusted protected floor with modeled adverse fills, explicit numeric
  momentum/retracement definitions, no timer resets or repeated extensions,
  and strictly as-of-breach evidence. Evaluate net expectancy, drawdown,
  false-hold/adverse-move rate, and include later-decline counterexamples;
  reserve an out-of-sample period before any implementation decision.
- Final protocol controls: calculate TP progress only from immutable original
  entry and original TP; exclude and report zero/negative/invalid target
  distance. Every A/B/C candidate must use identical historical data,
  evaluation periods, and fee/slippage assumptions. Candidate B must model an
  intrawindow price path sufficiently to determine which of its emergency
  floor or target was reached first; candle high/low alone is not evidence of
  that order. Pre-register the minimum sample size, required net-expectancy
  improvement, and maximum allowed drawdown deterioration before results are
  viewed, then report the qualification count for the B cohort.
- Final cohort and comparison definition: Candidate B is eligible only in the
  85% to strictly-less-than-90% original-TP-progress band, with fresh,
  quality-valid 1h `NEUTRAL`, supportive fresh/quality-valid 5m, and required
  current-frame structure/momentum evidence. Missing, stale, invalid, or
  unknown 1h/5m evidence makes the case ineligible rather than non-bearish.
  This avoids changing or competing with existing FIX-11T behaviour at 90%.
  Pre-count the eligible cohort; insufficient sample means keep collecting.
  B-versus-A is the sole primary comparison; C is secondary/exploratory and
  must have its trailing formula fixed before Replay. Verify ATR, spread, and
  tick-size availability as of breach; if historical spread is unavailable,
  use a declared fixed assumption and sensitivity analysis.
- Final breach semantics: eligibility uses `breach_tp_progress`, calculated
  from the executable price at the exact profit-lock breach, not the historic
  high-water mark. Persist/report both `max_tp_progress` and
  `breach_tp_progress`. The continuation emergency floor is distinct from the
  breached original lock; if actual executable price (including modeled
  fee/spread/slippage) already violates that floor, exit immediately. Freeze
  numerical precision, 90%-boundary precedence (FIX-11T owns 90%+), hold
  length, tolerance, floor, costs, sample threshold, and success criteria
  before inspecting results. Report confidence intervals and chronological
  out-of-sample results; a small qualifying cohort is insufficient evidence.
- Team-2 final validity controls: create and commit a numerical pre-registration
  before any outcome is viewed, recording its immutable content hash here.
  Verify available intrabar path data; where tick/price-event evidence cannot
  order TP versus emergency-floor contact, mark the case ambiguous, apply one
  conservative adverse-move-first rule consistently, and report analyses both
  with and without those cases. Compute the floor per trade as
  `max(original_lock - tolerance, fee_adjusted_breakeven)` and report when
  breakeven binds. Compare A and B as paired outcomes on the same eligible
  breach events, reporting per-event B-minus-A and the count of later declines
  avoided by A.

# Replay-versus-Production parity audit Г?" 2026-10-10

- Scope: source-level comparison of the manual Regression/Replay pipeline with
  the current paper-trading Production path. No trading code, configuration,
  database data, or Replay result was changed.
- Confirmed shared authority: Replay regenerates technical indicators through
  `TechnicalIndicatorService`, signals through `AnalysisService`, BUY authority
  through `ExecutionIntelligenceService`, BUY/SELL validation through
  `TradeExecutionValidationService`, and normal exit, TP-continuation,
  profit-lock, and near-TP policies through the corresponding shared policy
  classes. When canonical `LEGACY`/`APPLIED` price events exist, the Replay
  consumes them in timestamp order before making the same close-candle signal
  visible, matching the documented live ordering.
- Confirmed boundary: Replay is a shadow simulation, not a transactionally
  identical copy of Production. It starts with fixed capital of 10,000 USDT,
  manages one symbol/position in isolation, uses replay-local daily budget and
  position state, and writes only `*_test` tables. It therefore cannot
  reproduce an actual wallet balance, simultaneous positions, global
  `maxOpenPositions`, daily loss limit, idempotency races, database locks, or
  real execution latency.
- Confirmed historical-evidence limitation: derivatives history is not
  persisted, so historical Replay always receives `UNAVAILABLE` derivatives
  context when that feature is enabled. Historical order-book evaluation uses
  stored snapshots only and becomes `UNAVAILABLE` when no snapshot exists,
  whereas live Production samples its in-memory/live Binance source. Such runs
  are useful simulations but are not full signal-parity proof unless the
  required historical evidence exists and is reported.
- Confirmed price-evidence limitation: a Replay run labels itself
  `EXACT_PRICE_REPLAY` whenever at least one accepted price event exists. It
  does not prove complete tick coverage for every time an open position needed
  protection. Windows with no accepted events fall back to signal prices and
  are labelled `SIGNAL_PRICE_FALLBACK`; neither fallback nor partial coverage
  should be described as tick-exact reproduction.
- Test evidence: Java 21 focused tests passed for profit-lock/TP-extension
  parity, position exit/continuation, near-TP policy, and shared sizing.
  Existing Replay-specific coverage is narrow: it does not execute a complete
  Production-versus-Replay scenario across entry, add, wallet controls,
  price-event ordering, partial harvest, and exit.
- Conclusion: Replay is suitable for controlled, manual strategy regression
  and the approved FIX-155 research work only when each run carries explicit
  evidence-quality labels. It is not yet valid to claim it is fully identical
  to Production in all phases. A future parity program must add deterministic
  scenario tests and a run-level eligibility contract before using Replay as a
  release-equivalence certificate.


===== codex-docs/TRADE_INVESTIGATION_AGENT.md =====
# Trade Investigation Agent

## Role

Investigate one Crypto AI Trader trade from durable evidence. Explain what
happened, why the system entered and exited, whether it followed the intended
rules, and whether the entry/exit was supported by the information available
at that time.

Investigations are read-only. Do not change code, configuration, services,
database records, review states, Replay work, or deployment state.

## Required input

Obtain or resolve:

- Environment and application: local, test, or production.
- Database host, port, and schema.
- One trade reference: `paper_position.id`, `wallet_trade.id`, or a symbol plus
  a bounded approximate entry-time window.
- Optional question or suspected issue.

Before analysis, confirm the symbol, trade/position ID, running version or FIX
level, and ingestion mode. Read the actual schema before choosing tables or
columns. Do not assume a table is in `crypto_ai` when it may belong to
`crypto_ai_v2`.

## Evidence rules

- Use configured database and SSH/log connections without displaying secrets.
- Use bounded, read-only queries and targeted log searches.
- Treat stored timestamps as UTC; display both UTC and KSA (`UTC+03:00`).
- Attach record IDs and timestamped log evidence to every factual conclusion.
- Distinguish **confirmed fact**, **supported inference**, and **missing or
  conflicting evidence**.
- Missing logs or records do not prove that no execution/effect occurred.
- A saved signal does not prove execution. A callback return does not prove a
  transaction committed. Under FIX-153, `market_price_event.APPLIED` proves a
  committed successful protection callback, but can be a no-op when there was
  no open position; find the position/trade mutation separately.
- Current configuration does not prove which settings were active at entry. If
  no configuration history exists, report that limitation.

## Investigation workflow

### 1. Resolve identity and environment

Link the trade reference to, where available:

- `paper_position`
- `wallet_trade`
- entry and exit `trade_signal`
- `signal_processing_work`
- wallet/position execution records

Record entry and exit times in UTC and KSA. Confirm whether the trader ran in
local-candle OFF mode or a legacy shared-market mode.

### 2. Collect a small evidence bundle

Start with exact IDs and a narrow time window. Expand only when the evidence
requires it. Collect:

| Evidence | Establishes |
| --- | --- |
| `paper_position` | lifecycle, entry/exit, stop, target, status |
| `wallet_trade` | executed quantity, prices, fees, linked signal |
| `trade_signal` | decision, strategy, scores, entry eligibility, reasons |
| `signal_processing_work` | claim, attempt, completion, expiry, uncertainty |
| `candle` / `technical_indicator` | market data and analysis inputs available then |
| `market_price_event` | intra-candle protection observations and outcomes |
| configuration/version evidence | applicable rules, when historically available |
| application and timing logs | analysis, execution, protection, exceptions, waits |

Avoid fetching large signal explanation fields until they are needed.

### 3. Reconstruct the timeline

Build a timeline, explicitly showing missing stages:

`candle closed Г+' signal saved Г+' work claimed Г+' locks acquired Г+' entry committed Г+' protection observations Г+' exit decision Г+' exit committed`

Measure separately:

- candle close to signal generation;
- signal generation to processing/entry;
- observation time to protection completion;
- exit-trigger evidence to recorded exit.

Correlate delays only with logs/events for the same symbol, position, signal,
or price-event identity. A nearby slow SQL query is not proof of causation.

### 4. Assess entry correctness and quality

Use only evidence that was available at entry. Check:

- signal mode, closed-candle identity, freshness, and data quality;
- final decision and entry-allowed result;
- BTC context, higher-timeframe, ATR, liquidity, risk/reward, sizing, and
  strategy gates that applied at that time;
- correct signal/position linkage and absence of duplicate/stale-retry entry;
- quantity, wallet balance, risk limits, fees, and fill/reference-price gap.

Return two separate conclusions:

- **Execution correctness:** followed or violated the implemented rules.
- **Entry quality:** supported, weak, or unverifiable from contemporaneous
  evidence.

Subsequent price movement may evaluate the outcome; it must never be presented
as information the entry decision had.

### 5. Reconstruct protection and stop-loss behavior

For FIX-153, inspect accepted `LEGACY`/`APPLIED` price events and retain
`PENDING`, `NON_CANONICAL`, and `PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED` records as evidence
gaps or exclusions. Never substitute candle OHLC for absent intra-candle price
evidence.

For each relevant accepted observation:

1. Determine the then-active stop, target, trailing stop, and profit-lock state.
2. Find the first accepted observation that met an exit threshold.
3. Confirm that protection evaluated it and identify the resulting position or
   wallet execution record.
4. Explain delays, rejections, stale/out-of-order classification, exceptions,
   lock waits, or pool pressure.

Classify the exit path accurately: fixed stop-loss, trailing stop, profit-lock,
take-profit, signal-driven exit, manual exit, or unknown.

### 6. Assess exit correctness and quality

Compare the recorded exit reason, quantity, and price with trigger evidence.
Check threshold reach, accepted-observation identity, protection timing, and
the committed execution. Calculate maximum favourable/adverse movement during
the holding period separately from the contemporaneous exit assessment.

### 7. Reconcile financial results

Recalculate using execution records:

- bought/sold quantities and weighted entry/exit prices;
- gross P&L, fees, and net P&L;
- partial exits, remaining quantity, and realized versus unrealized values.

Flag discrepancies among wallet records, position records, and UI output.

### 8. Diagnose cause and use Replay carefully

Classify findings as one or more of:

- strategy decision;
- data freshness or coverage;
- analysis delay;
- execution failure;
- protection failure;
- database, lock, or connection-pool contention;
- accounting/UI discrepancy;
- insufficient evidence.

Run Replay only as a controlled comparison with equivalent candles, accepted
prices, strategy version, and settings. State every unavailable input or
material difference. Never claim parity by substituting candle OHLC for missing
price evidence or treating `PENDING` as accepted.

## Required final report

1. **Trade identity and environment** Г?" IDs, symbol, version/mode, scope.
2. **UTC/KSA timeline** Г?" evidence-backed sequence and latency breakdown.
3. **Entry** Г?" execution correctness and entry quality.
4. **Exit** Г?" execution correctness and exit quality.
5. **Stop-loss/profit-lock** Г?" worked, failed, or unverifiable, with evidence.
6. **P&L reconciliation** Г?" quantities, prices, fees, and discrepancies.
7. **Root cause and confidence** Г?" confirmed defect, valid loss, configuration
   issue, delay, or insufficient evidence.
8. **Evidence references** Г?" record IDs, query/log timestamps, code/config
   references.
9. **Missing evidence** Г?" exactly what prevents a stronger conclusion.
10. **Recommended correction and regression checks** Г?" only when justified.

A profitable trade can violate the rules, and a losing trade can be a correct
execution. State those independently.


===== codex-docs/TRADING_RULES.md =====
# FIX-154 higher-timeframe BUY rule Г?" 2026-10-10

- `data_quality_valid=true` is required before a bearish 1h signal can be
  treated as trusted. `NULL` is unknown and fails closed for bearish evidence.
- A trusted `STRONG_SELL` at `confidence_score >= 70` blocks the BUY.
- A trusted `SELL`, or trusted `STRONG_SELL` below 70, is a SOFT conflict:
  profiles receive effective 1h `NEUTRAL`, and any allowed initial BUY is
  capped at 25%.
- A bearish 5m still returns `5M_BEARISH_VETO` before any 1h result.
- Existing progressive/add rules retain their raw 1h requirement; a SOFT 1h
  never authorizes an add.

# FIX-153 candle continuity rule Г?" 2026-10-10

- Missing price evidence never becomes invented OHLCV data and never bypasses
  the data-quality gate.
- Automatic reconciliation may restore only one exact, final Binance candle.
  Its recovered/backfilled row has no close token and cannot directly cause
  analysis, a signal, or a trade.
- A later normal live close is the only route that resumes ordinary signal
  generation after a repaired quality window.

### DB_REQUEST

QueryId: DBQ-CODEX-I1-001  
Purpose: Confirm the actual current schema, columns, and foreign-key relationships before requesting the six frozen trade records and related execution/protection evidence.  
SQL:
```sql
SELECT
    c.table_name,
    c.ordinal_position,
    c.column_name,
    c.column_type,
    c.is_nullable,
    c.column_key,
    c.extra
FROM information_schema.columns c
WHERE c.table_schema = 'crypto_ai'
  AND c.table_name IN (
      'paper_position',
      'trade_signal',
      'wallet_trade',
      'wallet_managed_position',
      'market_price_event',
      'signal_processing_work'
  )
ORDER BY c.table_name, c.ordinal_position;

SELECT
    k.table_name,
    k.column_name,
    k.constraint_name,
    k.referenced_table_name,
    k.referenced_column_name
FROM information_schema.key_column_usage k
WHERE k.table_schema = 'crypto_ai'
  AND k.table_name IN (
      'paper_position',
      'trade_signal',
      'wallet_trade',
      'wallet_managed_position',
      'market_price_event',
      'signal_processing_work'
  )
  AND (
      k.referenced_table_name IS NOT NULL
      OR k.constraint_name = 'PRIMARY'
  )
ORDER BY k.table_name, k.constraint_name, k.ordinal_position;
```
### END_DB_REQUEST

### DB_REQUEST
QueryId: DBQ-CODEX-I3-001  
Purpose: Establish the six tradesƒ?T exact timing/correlation keys and inspect durable price/protection and processing evidence around each recorded exit. All returned timestamps are UTC; report conversions separately as KSA = UTC+3.  
SQL:
```sql
SHOW CREATE TABLE crypto_ai.market_price_event;
SHOW CREATE TABLE crypto_ai.signal_processing_work;

SELECT p.*
FROM crypto_ai.paper_position p
WHERE p.id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
ORDER BY FIELD(p.id, 182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137);

SELECT s.*
FROM crypto_ai.trade_signal s
JOIN crypto_ai.paper_position p
  ON s.id = p.signal_id OR s.id = p.exit_signal_id
WHERE p.id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
ORDER BY p.id, s.id;

SELECT w.*
FROM crypto_ai.signal_processing_work w
WHERE w.signal_id IN (
    SELECT p.signal_id
    FROM crypto_ai.paper_position p
    WHERE p.id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
    UNION
    SELECT p.exit_signal_id
    FROM crypto_ai.paper_position p
    WHERE p.id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
      AND p.exit_signal_id IS NOT NULL
)
ORDER BY w.signal_id, w.id;

SELECT
    p.id AS paper_position_id,
    m.*
FROM crypto_ai.paper_position p
JOIN crypto_ai.market_price_event m
  ON m.symbol = p.symbol
 AND m.observed_at >= DATE_SUB(p.closed_at, INTERVAL 10 MINUTE)
 AND m.observed_at <= DATE_ADD(p.closed_at, INTERVAL 2 MINUTE)
WHERE p.id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
ORDER BY p.id, m.observed_at, m.id;
```
### END_DB_REQUEST

### DB_REQUEST
QueryId: DBQ-CODEX-I4-001  
Purpose: Map each frozen paper position to its actual entry, live/signal exit route, wallet execution, managed position, exit audit, price-event evidence, signal work, and position-analysis evidence. All returned timestamps are UTC; report KSA only as UTC +03:00.  
SQL:
```sql
SHOW CREATE TABLE crypto_ai.paper_position;
SHOW CREATE TABLE crypto_ai.wallet_trade;
SHOW CREATE TABLE crypto_ai.wallet_managed_position;
SHOW CREATE TABLE crypto_ai.production_exit_audit;
SHOW CREATE TABLE crypto_ai.position_analysis;
SHOW CREATE TABLE crypto_ai.market_price_event;
SHOW CREATE TABLE crypto_ai.trade_signal;
SHOW CREATE TABLE crypto_ai.signal_processing_work;

SELECT
    p.id, p.symbol, p.side, p.status, p.quantity,
    p.entry_price, p.stop_loss, p.take_profit, p.exit_price, p.realized_pnl,
    p.signal_id, p.exit_signal_id, p.close_reason, p.entry_reason, p.exit_reason,
    p.opened_at, p.closed_at
FROM crypto_ai.paper_position p
WHERE p.id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
ORDER BY FIELD(p.id, 182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137);

SELECT
    a.*
FROM crypto_ai.production_exit_audit a
WHERE a.paper_position_id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
ORDER BY a.paper_position_id, a.audited_at, a.id;

SELECT
    p.id AS paper_position_id,
    m.*
FROM crypto_ai.paper_position p
JOIN crypto_ai.wallet_managed_position m
  ON m.entry_signal_id = p.signal_id
WHERE p.id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
ORDER BY p.id, m.opened_at, m.id;

SELECT
    p.id AS paper_position_id,
    w.*
FROM crypto_ai.paper_position p
LEFT JOIN crypto_ai.production_exit_audit a
  ON a.paper_position_id = p.id
JOIN crypto_ai.wallet_trade w
  ON w.signal_id = p.signal_id
  OR w.signal_id = p.exit_signal_id
  OR (
      a.wallet_position_id IS NOT NULL
      AND w.execution_key LIKE CONCAT('POSITION:', a.wallet_position_id, ':%')
  )
  OR (
      a.position_analysis_id IS NOT NULL
      AND w.execution_key = CONCAT('POSITION_ANALYSIS:', a.position_analysis_id, ':STOP_LOSS')
  )
WHERE p.id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
ORDER BY p.id, w.executed_at, w.id;

SELECT
    refs.paper_position_id,
    refs.signal_role,
    s.id, s.symbol, s.interval_code, s.candle_open_time,
    s.decision, s.original_decision, s.final_entry_allowed,
    s.latest_price, s.stop_loss, s.take_profit,
    s.total_score, s.confidence_score, s.risk_reward_ratio,
    s.data_quality_valid, s.signal_mode, s.generated_at,
    s.explanation, s.final_decision_explanation, s.decision_path
FROM (
    SELECT p.id AS paper_position_id, 'ENTRY' AS signal_role, p.signal_id AS signal_id
    FROM crypto_ai.paper_position p
    WHERE p.id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)

    UNION ALL

    SELECT p.id, 'PAPER_EXIT_SIGNAL', p.exit_signal_id
    FROM crypto_ai.paper_position p
    WHERE p.id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
      AND p.exit_signal_id IS NOT NULL

    UNION ALL

    SELECT a.paper_position_id, 'LIVE_EXIT_CONTEXT', a.source_signal_id
    FROM crypto_ai.production_exit_audit a
    WHERE a.paper_position_id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
      AND a.source_signal_id IS NOT NULL
) refs
JOIN crypto_ai.trade_signal s ON s.id = refs.signal_id
ORDER BY refs.paper_position_id, refs.signal_role, s.id;

SELECT
    p.id AS paper_position_id,
    pa.*
FROM crypto_ai.paper_position p
JOIN crypto_ai.production_exit_audit a
  ON a.paper_position_id = p.id
JOIN crypto_ai.position_analysis pa
  ON pa.wallet_position_id = a.wallet_position_id
WHERE p.id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
ORDER BY p.id, pa.analyzed_at, pa.id;

SELECT
    p.id AS paper_position_id,
    w.*
FROM crypto_ai.paper_position p
JOIN crypto_ai.signal_processing_work w
  ON w.signal_id = p.signal_id
  OR w.signal_id = p.exit_signal_id
WHERE p.id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
ORDER BY p.id, w.signal_id, w.id;

SELECT
    p.id AS paper_position_id,
    e.id, e.symbol, e.observed_at, e.price, e.source,
    e.delivery_status, e.ordering_slot, e.outcome_reason, e.created_at
FROM crypto_ai.paper_position p
JOIN crypto_ai.market_price_event e
  ON e.symbol = p.symbol
 AND e.observed_at >= DATE_SUB(p.closed_at, INTERVAL 15 MINUTE)
 AND e.observed_at <= DATE_ADD(p.closed_at, INTERVAL 2 MINUTE)
WHERE p.id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
ORDER BY p.id, e.observed_at, e.id;
```

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/processing/SignalProcessingCoordinator.java =====
package com.crypto.execution.processing;

import com.crypto.domain.PaperPosition;
import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
import com.crypto.repository.TradeSignalRepository;
import com.crypto.wallet.repository.WalletManagedPositionRepository;
import com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.PlatformTransactionManager;
import org.springframework.transaction.TransactionDefinition;
import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionTemplate;
import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.function.Function;

/** FIX-127: one coordinator for all Production signal callers. No retry of the
 * existing body after it has begun; only the new initial lock is retry-marked. */
@Service
public class SignalProcessingCoordinator {
    // FIX-125 mandatory in Spring; null only in existing constructor-only policy tests.
    @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired
    private com.crypto.wallet.service.WalletTransactionCoordination walletCoordination;

    private static final Logger log=LoggerFactory.getLogger(SignalProcessingCoordinator.class);
    private final SignalProcessingStore store;
    private final TradeSignalRepository signals;
    private final WalletManagedPositionRepository positions;
    private final SignalProcessingFreshness freshness;
    private final TransactionTemplate transaction;
    public SignalProcessingCoordinator(SignalProcessingStore store, TradeSignalRepository signals,
            WalletManagedPositionRepository positions, SignalProcessingFreshness freshness, PlatformTransactionManager manager) {
        this.store=store;this.signals=signals;this.positions=positions;this.freshness=freshness;
        transaction=new TransactionTemplate(manager);
        transaction.setPropagationBehavior(TransactionDefinition.PROPAGATION_REQUIRES_NEW);
    }
    public Optional<PaperPosition> process(long signalId, boolean background,
            Function<TradeSignal,Optional<PaperPosition>> business) {
        return process(signalId,background,business,null);
    }
    public Optional<PaperPosition> process(long signalId, boolean background,
            Function<TradeSignal,Optional<PaperPosition>> business, Long sourceEventId) {
        if(TransactionSynchronizationManager.isActualTransactionActive())
            throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-127 caller must be outside the processing transaction");
        if(!background && !store.exists(signalId)) {
            // Automatic callers have already registered atomically with creation.
            // Explicit existing-signal calls register only at the user's request.
            transaction.executeWithoutResult(tx->store.register(signals.findById(signalId)
                    .orElseThrow(()->new IllegalArgumentException("Signal not found: "+signalId)),ProcessingOrigin.EXPLICIT));
        }
        store.requireSourceOwner(signalId,sourceEventId);
        for(int localAttempt=0;localAttempt<2;localAttempt++) {
            var work=store.claim(signalId,background);
            if(work==null) {
                log.info("[FIX-127][NOT_CLAIMED] signalId={}, background={}; no processing performed",signalId,background);
                return Optional.empty();
            }
            log.info("[FIX-127][ATTEMPT] signalId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, candleOpenTime={}, origin={}, attempt={}, background={}",
                    signalId,work.symbol(),work.interval(),work.candleOpenTime(),work.origin(),work.attempts(),background);
            java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger completion=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger(-1);
            try {
                Optional<PaperPosition> result=transaction.execute(tx->{
                    org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager.registerSynchronization(
                        new org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronization() {
                            @Override public void afterCompletion(int status) { completion.set(status); }
                        });
                    // FIX-140: observe the real transaction completion; a thrown exception alone
                    // does not prove rollback. The private marker is only created here.
                    if(walletCoordination!=null) {
                        try { walletCoordination.symbol(work.symbol()); }
                        catch(RuntimeException failure) {
                            if(sourceEventId!=null && retryableSymbolLock(failure))throw new SymbolLockFailure(failure);
                            throw failure;
                        }
                    }
                    var owned=store.locked(signalId);
                    if(owned==null || !"RUNNING".equals(owned.status()) || !work.owner().equals(owned.owner()))
                        throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-127 processing ownership changed");
                    // Acquire before PositionManagementService inserts a referencing
                    // advisory or executes POSITION_STOP_LOSS. No rules evaluated yet.
                    try {
                        positions.findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(work.symbol(),"OPEN");
                    } catch(RuntimeException ex) {
                        if(InitialPositionLockDeadlock.isMysqlDeadlock(ex))throw new InitialLockFailure(ex);
                        throw ex;
                    }
                    store.requireSourceOwner(signalId,sourceEventId);
                    // Read signal/freshness after lock acquisition: lock wait must not
                    // consume the recovery grace before it is checked, or establish an
                    // ordinary repeatable-read snapshot before the position is locked.
                    TradeSignal signal=signals.findById(signalId).orElseThrow(()->new IllegalArgumentException("Signal missing"));
                    if(!work.symbol().equals(signal.getSymbol()) || !work.interval().equals(signal.getInterval())
                            || !work.candleOpenTime().equals(signal.getCandleOpenTime()))
                        throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-127 signal lineage changed");
                    // FIX-153: every claimed attempt, including the first direct
                    // attempt regardless of origin, is checked after normal locks.
                    if(!freshness.eligible(work))throw new Expired();
                    Optional<PaperPosition> outcome=business.apply(signal);
                    store.complete(work,outcome.map(PaperPosition::getId).orElse(null));
                    return outcome;
                });
                log.info("[FIX-127][COMPLETED] signalId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, attempt={}; processing committed (not necessarily a trade)",
                        signalId,work.symbol(),work.interval(),work.attempts());
                return result;
            } catch(Expired ex) {
                // Only exact RUNNING ownership can expire this attempt, after business TX rolled back.
                if(store.finishWithoutBusiness(work,"EXPIRED","FRESHNESS","Exact candle is no longer latest and fresh")!=1)
                    throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 expiry ownership unconfirmed: signal="+signalId);
                log.warn("[FIX-127][EXPIRED] signalId={}, symbol={}, attempt={}",signalId,work.symbol(),work.attempts());
                return Optional.empty();
            } catch(RuntimeException ex) {
                // FIX-140: only this invocation's initial symbol-lock marker plus a
                // confirmed rollback grants retry. Unknown connection/commit failures stay review-only.
                if(ex instanceof SymbolLockFailure && completion.get()==
                        org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronization.STATUS_ROLLED_BACK) {
                    String result=work.attempts()<4?"SYMBOL_LOCK_RETRY":"LOCK_RETRY_EXHAUSTED";
                    int changed=store.finishSymbolLockFailure(work,result,ex.toString());
                    if(changed!=1)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-140 retry persistence unconfirmed",ex);
                    log.warn("[FIX-140][SAFE_RETRY_RESULT] event={}, signal={}, attempt={}, outcome={}, rollback=CONFIRMED",
                        sourceEventId,signalId,work.attempts(),result);
                    if("SYMBOL_LOCK_RETRY".equals(result))throw new SymbolLockRetryScheduled(signalId);
                    return Optional.empty();
                }
                boolean initial=ex instanceof InitialLockFailure;
                String status=initial && work.attempts()<2 ? "RETRYABLE_FAILURE" : "REVIEW_REQUIRED";
                int recorded=store.finishWithoutBusiness(work,status,initial ? "INITIAL_POSITION_LOCK" : "PROCESSING_OR_COMMIT",ex.toString());
                log.error("[FIX-127][{}] signalId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, attempt={}, initialLock={}",
                        recorded==1 ? status : "FAILURE_RECORD_NOT_UPDATED",signalId,work.symbol(),work.interval(),work.attempts(),initial,ex);
                if(!initial || work.attempts()>=2)throw ex;
                // TransactionTemplate completed rollback before entering this catch.
            }
        }
        return Optional.empty();
    }
    // FIX-140: classify only known lock cancellation/deadlock errors. Communications
    // errors are deliberately excluded even when nested below a data-access exception.
    static boolean retryableSymbolLock(Throwable failure) {
        for(Throwable e=failure;e!=null;e=e.getCause())
            if(e instanceof java.sql.SQLException sql && sql.getSQLState()!=null && sql.getSQLState().startsWith("08"))return false;
        for(Throwable e=failure;e!=null;e=e.getCause())
            if(e instanceof org.springframework.dao.QueryTimeoutException ||
               e instanceof java.sql.SQLTimeoutException ||
               e instanceof java.sql.SQLException sql && (sql.getErrorCode()==1205 || sql.getErrorCode()==1213))return true;
        return false;
    }
    private static final class SymbolLockFailure extends RuntimeException {
        SymbolLockFailure(Throwable cause) { super(cause); }
    }
    // A private marker cannot be supplied by a later business callback.
    private static final class InitialLockFailure extends RuntimeException {
        InitialLockFailure(RuntimeException cause) { super(cause); }
    }
    private static final class Expired extends RuntimeException {}
}


===== src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java =====
package com.crypto.service;

import com.crypto.domain.BtcContextStatus;
import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
import com.crypto.repository.TradeSignalRepository;
import com.crypto.execution.service.ExecutionReplayScope;
import com.crypto.execution.service.EntryConsumptionPolicy;
import com.crypto.execution.domain.EntryConsumptionState;
import com.crypto.wallet.domain.ExecutionProfile;
import com.crypto.wallet.domain.WalletSettings;
import com.crypto.wallet.repository.WalletSettingsRepository;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;

/**
 * Execution-only multi-timeframe validation.
 *
 * Frozen responsibilities:
 *  - 1m: the only normal execution timeframe
 *  - 5m: tactical confirmation
 *  - 1h: strategic filter/veto
 *
 * Execution profiles affect only entry eligibility and position percentage.
 * They never modify indicators, scores, or FinalDecisionService output.
 */
@Slf4j
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class TradeExecutionValidationService {

    private static final String EXECUTION_INTERVAL = "1m";
    private static final String CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL = "5m";
    private static final String TREND_INTERVAL = "1h";
    private static final Duration ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofMinutes(10);
    private static final Duration FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofMinutes(20);
    private static final Duration ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofHours(3);

    // FIX-11G: this mirrors the existing global CHASE_ENTRY_CUTOFF in
    // ExecutionIntelligenceService. We deliberately reuse the proven execution-quality
    // boundary instead of inventing a new confidence/quality threshold for this one route.
    // The downstream global Entry Quality guard still remains authoritative and re-checks it.
    private static final int BALANCED_TRANSITIONAL_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY = 50;

    private final TradeSignalRepository signalRepository;
    private final WalletSettingsRepository settingsRepository;
    private final EntryConsumptionPolicy entryConsumptionPolicy;
    private final HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService htfAssessment;
    @Autowired(required = false)
    private ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
    @Autowired(required = false)
    private BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore htfAuditStore;
    @Autowired(required = false)
    private Fix154Metrics fix154Metrics;

    public ValidationResult validateBuy(TradeSignal executionSignal, int entryQualityScore) {
        ValidationResult structural = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, true);
        if (!structural.allowed()) return structural;

        WalletSettings settings = settings();
        if (settings.isRequireNewBuyTransition()) {
            TradeSignal previous = previousSignal(executionSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
            // FIX-112A: a bullish label alone is not a consumed entry. Only keep
            // BUY_CONTINUATION protection when that exact previous signal has an
            // executed BUY (Production) or executed shadow BUY (Replay).
            if (isFresh(previous, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE)
                    && isBullish(previous.getDecision())
                    && entryConsumptionPolicy.resolve(previous.getId()) != EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED) {
                return ValidationResult.reject("BUY_CONTINUATION",
                        "BUY was not executed because a recent bullish opportunity for this symbol was already consumed. "
                                + "A new WATCH/NEUTRAL/SELL -> BUY transition is required.");
            }
        }

        TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
        if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
            return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
                    "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
        }

        TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
        if (!isFresh(oneHour, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
            return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
                    "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
        }

        HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
        if(!assessment.allowed()) {
            recordAssessmentMetric(assessment);
            audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
            return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
        }

        ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings.getExecutionProfile());
        ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
            case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
            // FIX-112B: only the fresh direct-BUY BALANCED path receives the narrow
            // 5m-NEUTRAL exception. validateBuyContext() and AGGRESSIVE fallback keep
            // calling balancedBuy() directly so their proven Production behavior is unchanged.
            case BALANCED -> balancedBuyWithNeutralFiveException(
                    executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour(), entryQualityScore);
            case AGGRESSIVE -> aggressiveBuy(executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
        };
        ValidationResult result = cap(profileResult, assessment);
        audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, result.code(), result.explanation());
        return result;
    }

    /**
     * FIX-021 / accumulated-evidence authority parity:
     * Validate only the current 5m/1h execution authority using the same configured
     * execution profile as a normal direct BUY. This intentionally skips the 1m BUY
     * transition requirement because accumulated evidence may be triggered by a WATCH,
     * but it must never be allowed to use weaker higher-timeframe authority than the
     * normal BUY path would accept.
     */
    public ValidationResult validateBuyContext(TradeSignal referenceSignal) {
        return validateBuyContext(referenceSignal, "ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE");
    }

    /** The caller path is audit evidence only; context eligibility remains identical. */
    public ValidationResult validateBuyContext(TradeSignal referenceSignal, String auditPath) {
        if (referenceSignal == null || referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
            return ValidationResult.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Execution reference signal is missing required timing data.");
        }

        TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
        if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
            return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
                    "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for accumulated-evidence authority.");
        }

        TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
        if (!isFresh(oneHour, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
            return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
                    "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for accumulated-evidence authority.");
        }

        HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
        if(!assessment.allowed()) {
            recordAssessmentMetric(assessment);
            audit(referenceSignal, auditPath, assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY context.");
            return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY context.");
        }

        ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings().getExecutionProfile());
        TradeSignal oneMinuteQuality = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, EXECUTION_INTERVAL);
        TradeSignal qualitySignal = oneMinuteQuality == null ? referenceSignal : oneMinuteQuality;
        ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
            case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
            case BALANCED -> balancedBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
            case AGGRESSIVE -> aggressiveBuy(qualitySignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
        };
        ValidationResult result = cap(profileResult, assessment);
        audit(referenceSignal, auditPath, assessment, result.code(), result.explanation());
        return result;
    }

    public ValidationResult validateSell(TradeSignal executionSignal) {
        ValidationResult base = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, false);
        if (!base.allowed()) return base;

        TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
        if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
            return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
                    "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m SELL signal.");
        }
        if (!isBearish(fiveMinute.getDecision())) {
            return ValidationResult.reject("5M_NOT_BEARISH",
                    "1m SELL was blocked because the fresh 5m decision was " + fiveMinute.getDecision() + ".");
        }

        TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
        if (!isFresh(oneHour, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
            return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
                    "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for the 1m SELL signal.");
        }
        if (isBullish(oneHour.getDecision())) {
            return ValidationResult.reject("1H_BULLISH_VETO",
                    "1m SELL was blocked because the fresh 1h strategic decision was " + oneHour.getDecision() + ".");
        }

        return ValidationResult.allow(100, "SELL_CONFIRMED",
                "1m SELL confirmed by fresh bearish 5m context; 1h is not bullish.");
    }

    private ValidationResult conservativeBuy(SignalDecision fiveMinute, SignalDecision oneHour) {
        if (!isBullish(fiveMinute)) {
            return ValidationResult.reject("5M_NOT_BULLISH",
                    "Conservative profile requires 5m BUY/STRONG_BUY; fresh 5m is " + fiveMinute + ".");
        }
        return ValidationResult.allow(100, "CONSERVATIVE_FULL",
                "Conservative profile approved a full-size entry: 1m BUY, bullish 5m confirmation, and non-bearish 1h context.");
    }

    /**
     * FIX-112B / Production behavior change:
     * 5m NEUTRAL means short-term confirmation is absent, but it is not bearish
     * opposition. After the existing BALANCED rules reject, allow only a small
     * exploratory entry when the fresh 1m BUY is high-confidence, 1h is genuinely
     * bullish, independent Entry Quality is strong, and BTC has no conflict state.
     *
     * Golden rule: Replay = Production. This method lives in the shared Production
     * validation service and Replay must call this exact path with historical inputs;
     * there is no Replay-only copy, threshold, or behavior fork.
     *
     * balancedBuy() intentionally remains unchanged. Existing BALANCED_FULL,
     * BALANCED_STRONG and BALANCED_EARLY authority always wins before this exception.
     * FIX-11G extends this same direct-BUY-only wrapper after FIX-112B with the distinct
     * 5m=NEUTRAL + 1h=WATCH transitional authority documented below.
     */
    private ValidationResult balancedBuyWithNeutralFiveException(
            TradeSignal executionSignal, SignalDecision fiveMinute, SignalDecision oneHour, int entryQualityScore) {
        ValidationResult balanced = balancedBuy(fiveMinute, oneHour);
        if (balanced.allowed()) return balanced;

        boolean neutralFiveBullishOne = fiveMinute == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL && isBullish(oneHour);
        BtcContextStatus btcStatus = executionSignal.getBtcContextStatus();
        // FIX-112B: plain/strong BTC conflict cannot be rescued by this exception.
        // FIX-141: nor can a failed freshness guard - STALE_CONTEXT is a real, evidenced
        // block (missing/stale/future-dated/unknown-candle-time BTC context), not an
        // "unavailable, non-authoritative" state. This exception must not restore the
        // entry permission the freshness guard already withheld upstream.
        // Other BTC states retain their existing upstream Production semantics.
        boolean btcSafeForException = btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.CONFLICT
                && btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.STRONG_CONFLICT
                && btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.STALE_CONTEXT;
        boolean strongEnough = executionSignal.getConfidenceScore() >= 72
                && entryQualityScore >= 70
                && btcSafeForException;

        if (neutralFiveBullishOne && strongEnough) {
            return ValidationResult.allow(25, "BALANCED_NEUTRAL_5M",
                    "BALANCED_NEUTRAL_5M: 25% exploratory entry approved because 5m is NEUTRAL "
                             + "rather than bearish while 1h remains " + oneHour
                            + ". 1m=" + executionSignal.getDecision()
                            + ", confidence=" + executionSignal.getConfidenceScore()
                             + ", 5m=NEUTRAL, 1h=" + oneHour
                            + ", entryQuality=" + entryQualityScore
                            + ", btcStatus=" + btcStatus
                            + ", position=25%.");
        }

        // FIX-11G / controlled Production behavior change:
        // An already-approved fresh 1m BUY can appear before tactical 5m confirmation
        // catches up. When 5m is still NEUTRAL and 1h is WATCH, neither timeframe is
        // bearish, but the original BALANCED matrix has no authority for this transition.
        // Grant only a 25% exploratory authority and only when FinalDecision already
        // allowed entry, BTC has no hard conflict, and Entry Quality is at/above the
        // existing chase cutoff. Do NOT add a second confidence threshold here: the
        // upstream BUY + finalEntryAllowed decision already owns signal confidence.
        //
        // Golden rule: Replay = Production. Replay reaches this exact shared method
        // through ExecutionReplayScope historical 5m/1h lookups; there is no Replay-only
        // copy, threshold, or special execution behavior. validateBuyContext() remains
        // intentionally unchanged so accumulated-evidence/recovery authority is not widened.
        boolean neutralFiveWatchOne = fiveMinute == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL && oneHour == SignalDecision.WATCH;
        if (neutralFiveWatchOne) {
            boolean upstreamApproved = executionSignal.isFinalEntryAllowed();
            boolean entryQualitySafe = entryQualityScore >= BALANCED_TRANSITIONAL_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY;
            // FIX-141: see btcSafeForException above - STALE_CONTEXT must not be rescued here either.
            boolean btcSafeForTransitionalAuthority = btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.CONFLICT
                    && btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.STRONG_CONFLICT
                    && btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.STALE_CONTEXT;

            if (upstreamApproved && entryQualitySafe && btcSafeForTransitionalAuthority) {
                log.info("FIX-11G transitional BUY authority granted: signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, "
                                + "decision={}, confidence={}, 5m={}, 1h={}, entryQuality={}, btcStatus={}, positionPercent=25",
                        executionSignal.getId(), executionSignal.getSymbol(), executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(),
                        executionSignal.getDecision(), executionSignal.getConfidenceScore(),
                        fiveMinute, oneHour, entryQualityScore, btcStatus);
                return ValidationResult.allow(25, "BALANCED_NEUTRAL_5M_WATCH_1H",
                        "BALANCED_NEUTRAL_5M_WATCH_1H: 25% exploratory entry approved for an already-approved "
                                + "1m BUY while 5m is NEUTRAL and 1h is WATCH. "
                                + "entryQuality=" + entryQualityScore
                                + ", btcStatus=" + btcStatus
                                + ", position=25%.");
            }

            // INFO is intentional here: this exact transition is the new FIX-11G boundary.
            // Keeping both grant and denial searchable by FIX-11G makes Production misses
            // diagnosable from application logs without enabling global DEBUG logging.
            log.info("FIX-11G transitional BUY authority not granted: signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, "
                            + "decision={}, confidence={}, finalEntryAllowed={}, 5m={}, 1h={}, entryQuality={}, "
                            + "minEntryQuality={}, btcStatus={}",
                    executionSignal.getId(), executionSignal.getSymbol(), executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(),
                    executionSignal.getDecision(), executionSignal.getConfidenceScore(), upstreamApproved,
                    fiveMinute, oneHour, entryQualityScore,
                    BALANCED_TRANSITIONAL_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY, btcStatus);
        }
        return balanced;
    }

    private ValidationResult balancedBuy(SignalDecision five, SignalDecision one) {

        if (isBullish(five) && isBullish(one)) {
            return ValidationResult.allow(100, "BALANCED_FULL",
                    "Balanced profile approved 100%: both 5m and 1h are bullish.");
        }
        if (isBullish(five) && isWatchOrNeutral(one)) {
            return ValidationResult.allow(75, "BALANCED_STRONG",
                    "Balanced profile approved 75%: 5m is bullish while 1h is " + one + ".");
        }
        if (five == SignalDecision.WATCH && isBullish(one)) {
            return ValidationResult.allow(75, "BALANCED_STRONG",
                    "Balanced profile approved 75%: 1h is bullish while 5m is WATCH.");
        }
        if (five == SignalDecision.WATCH && one == SignalDecision.WATCH) {
            return ValidationResult.allow(50, "BALANCED_EARLY",
                    "Balanced profile approved 50%: both 5m and 1h are WATCH, so this is an early reduced entry.");
        }

        return ValidationResult.reject("BALANCED_CONFIRMATION_INSUFFICIENT",
                "Balanced profile requires bullish/WATCH confirmation. Current 5m=" + five + ", 1h=" + one + ".");
    }

    private ValidationResult aggressiveBuy(TradeSignal executionSignal, SignalDecision five, SignalDecision one) {
        ValidationResult balanced = balancedBuy(five, one);
        if (balanced.allowed()) return balanced;

        boolean probeAlignment = five == SignalDecision.WATCH && one == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL;
        if (!probeAlignment) {
            return ValidationResult.reject("AGGRESSIVE_CONFIRMATION_INSUFFICIENT",
                    "Aggressive profile still requires 5m WATCH or better and a non-bearish 1h context. Current 5m="
                            + five + ", 1h=" + one + ".");
        }

        int confidence = executionSignal.getConfidenceScore();
        int trend = executionSignal.getTrendScore();
        int volume = executionSignal.getVolumeScore();
        int momentum = executionSignal.getMomentumScore();

        if (confidence < 85 || trend < 22 || volume < 16 || momentum < 12) {
            return ValidationResult.reject("AGGRESSIVE_PROBE_QUALITY",
                    "Aggressive 25% probe requires confidence>=85, trend>=22, volume>=16, momentum>=12. "
                            + "Actual confidence=" + confidence + ", trend=" + trend + ", volume=" + volume
                            + ", momentum=" + momentum + ".");
        }

        return ValidationResult.allow(25, "AGGRESSIVE_PROBE",
                "Aggressive profile approved a 25% probe: 1m BUY is high quality, 5m is WATCH, and 1h is NEUTRAL.");
    }

    private ValidationResult validateBaseSignal(TradeSignal signal, boolean buy) {
        if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
            return ValidationResult.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Execution signal is missing required timing data.");
        }
        if (!EXECUTION_INTERVAL.equals(signal.getInterval())) {
            return ValidationResult.reject("NON_EXECUTION_TIMEFRAME",
                    "Only 1m signals may trigger normal wallet execution. " + signal.getInterval() + " is context-only.");
        }
        if (buy && !isBullish(signal.getDecision())) {
            return ValidationResult.reject("NOT_BUY", "The 1m decision is not BUY/STRONG_BUY.");
        }
        if (!buy && !isBearish(signal.getDecision())) {
            return ValidationResult.reject("NOT_SELL", "The 1m decision is not SELL/STRONG_SELL.");
        }
        return ValidationResult.allow(100, "BASE_VALID", "Base execution signal is valid.");
    }

    private TradeSignal latestAtOrBefore(TradeSignal signal, String interval) {
        return latestSignal(signal.getSymbol(), interval, signal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
    }

    private java.util.Optional<TradeSignal> previousSignal(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
        if (replayScope != null && replayScope.active()) return replayScope.previousBefore(symbol, interval, reference);
        return signalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndGeneratedAtLessThanOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, interval, reference);
    }

    private java.util.Optional<TradeSignal> latestSignal(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
        if (replayScope != null && replayScope.active()) return replayScope.latestAtOrBefore(symbol, interval, reference);
        return signalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, interval, reference);
    }

    private boolean isFresh(TradeSignal signal, Instant reference, Duration maximumAge) {
        return signal != null
                && signal.getGeneratedAt() != null
                && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference)
                && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference.minus(maximumAge));
    }

    private boolean isBullish(SignalDecision decision) {
        return decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY;
    }

    private boolean isBearish(SignalDecision decision) {
        return decision == SignalDecision.SELL || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL;
    }

    private boolean isWatchOrNeutral(SignalDecision decision) {
        return decision == SignalDecision.WATCH || decision == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL;
    }

    private WalletSettings settings() {
        return settingsRepository.findById(1L)
                .orElseGet(() -> WalletSettings.builder()
                        .id(1L)
                        .executionProfile(ExecutionProfile.BALANCED.name())
                        .requireNewBuyTransition(true)
                        .dynamicProfitLockEnabled(true)
                        .profitLockActivationPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(70))
                        .profitLockInitialPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(40))
                        .profitLockTrailStepPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(10))
                        .build());
    }

    private ValidationResult cap(ValidationResult result, HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment) {
        if (!result.allowed() || assessment.maxInitialPercent()==null) return result;
        int capped=Math.min(result.positionPercent(),assessment.maxInitialPercent());
        if (capped != result.positionPercent() && fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.softCappedEntry();
        return capped==result.positionPercent()?result:ValidationResult.allow(capped,result.code(),result.explanation()+" FIX-154 soft-HTF cap="+capped+"%.");
    }

    /** Used only after progressive raw-HTF checks have already failed; never changes an add decision. */
    public void recordProgressiveAssessmentIfSoftBlocked(TradeSignal referenceSignal) {
        if (referenceSignal == null || referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
        TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
        TradeSignal one = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
        if (!isFresh(five, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)
                || !isFresh(one, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) return;
        HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment = htfAssessment.assess(five, true, one, true);
        if (assessment.band() == HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Band.SOFT_CONFLICT) {
            if (fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.softAddBlocked();
            audit(referenceSignal, "PROGRESSIVE_ADD", assessment, "SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED",
                    "Existing raw 1h progressive-add guard blocked this add; FIX-154 audit only.");
        }
    }

    private void audit(TradeSignal referenceSignal, String path,
                       HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment, String finalResult, String reason) {
        if (htfAuditStore == null) return;
        try {
            htfAuditStore.record(referenceSignal, path, assessment, finalResult, reason);
        } catch (RuntimeException auditFailure) {
            if (fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.auditWriteFailed();
            log.warn("FIX-154 audit handoff failed; BUY decision remains unchanged: path={}, signalId={}",
                    path, referenceSignal == null ? null : referenceSignal.getId(), auditFailure);
        }
    }

    private void recordAssessmentMetric(HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment) {
        if (fix154Metrics != null && "1H_BEARISH_VETO".equals(assessment.code())
                && assessment.band() == HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Band.NOT_TRUSTED) {
            fix154Metrics.untrustedBearishOneHourBlocked();
        }
    }

    public record ValidationResult(boolean allowed, String code, String explanation, int positionPercent) {
        public static ValidationResult allow(int positionPercent, String code, String explanation) {
            return new ValidationResult(true, code, explanation, Math.max(1, Math.min(100, positionPercent)));
        }

        public static ValidationResult reject(String code, String explanation) {
            return new ValidationResult(false, code, explanation, 0);
        }
    }
}


===== src/main/java/com/crypto/service/HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.java =====
package com.crypto.service;

import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

/**
 * Pure FIX-154 BUY-context assessment. It deliberately returns effective
 * decisions separately and never mutates the JPA-managed context signals.
 */
@Service
public class HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService {
    public static final int HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD = 70;

    public Assessment assess(TradeSignal fiveMinute, boolean fiveFresh,
                             TradeSignal oneHour, boolean oneFresh) {
        if (!fiveFresh) return Assessment.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION", Band.NOT_APPLICABLE, fiveMinute, oneHour);
        if (!oneFresh) return Assessment.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT", Band.NOT_APPLICABLE, fiveMinute, oneHour);

        SignalDecision five = decision(fiveMinute);
        SignalDecision one = decision(oneHour);
        if (bearish(five)) {
            boolean hard = hard(oneHour);
            return Assessment.reject("5M_BEARISH_VETO", hard ? Band.HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE : band(oneHour), fiveMinute, oneHour)
                    .withHardConfirmation(hard);
        }
        if (bearish(one) && !qualityValid(oneHour)) {
            return Assessment.reject("1H_BEARISH_VETO", Band.NOT_TRUSTED, fiveMinute, oneHour);
        }
        if (hard(oneHour)) {
            return Assessment.reject("HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO", Band.HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE, fiveMinute, oneHour);
        }
        if (soft(oneHour)) {
            return Assessment.allow(Band.SOFT_CONFLICT, five, SignalDecision.NEUTRAL, 25, fiveMinute, oneHour);
        }
        return Assessment.allow(qualityValid(oneHour) ? Band.NO_CONFLICT : Band.NOT_TRUSTED,
                five, one, null, fiveMinute, oneHour);
    }

    private Band band(TradeSignal one) {
        if (!qualityValid(one)) return Band.NOT_TRUSTED;
        if (hard(one)) return Band.HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE;
        if (soft(one)) return Band.SOFT_CONFLICT;
        return Band.NO_CONFLICT;
    }
    private boolean hard(TradeSignal s) { return qualityValid(s) && decision(s) == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL && confidence(s) >= HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD; }
    private boolean soft(TradeSignal s) { return qualityValid(s) && (decision(s) == SignalDecision.SELL || (decision(s) == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL && confidence(s) < HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD)); }
    private boolean qualityValid(TradeSignal s) { return s != null && Boolean.TRUE.equals(s.getDataQualityValid()); }
    private int confidence(TradeSignal s) { return s == null ? 0 : s.getConfidenceScore(); }
    private SignalDecision decision(TradeSignal s) { return s == null || s.getDecision() == null ? SignalDecision.NEUTRAL : s.getDecision(); }
    private boolean bearish(SignalDecision d) { return d == SignalDecision.SELL || d == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL; }

    public enum Band { NOT_APPLICABLE, NOT_TRUSTED, SOFT_CONFLICT, HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE, NO_CONFLICT }
    public record Assessment(boolean allowed, String code, Band band, SignalDecision effectiveFiveMinute,
                             SignalDecision effectiveOneHour, Integer maxInitialPercent,
                             boolean hardConfirmationPresent, TradeSignal rawFiveMinute, TradeSignal rawOneHour) {
        static Assessment reject(String code, Band band, TradeSignal five, TradeSignal one) {
            return new Assessment(false, code, band, decisionOf(five), decisionOf(one), null, false, five, one);
        }
        static Assessment allow(Band band, SignalDecision five, SignalDecision one, Integer cap, TradeSignal rawFive, TradeSignal rawOne) {
            return new Assessment(true, "HTF_CONTEXT_ALLOWED", band, five, one, cap, false, rawFive, rawOne);
        }
        Assessment withHardConfirmation(boolean value) { return new Assessment(allowed, code, band, effectiveFiveMinute, effectiveOneHour, maxInitialPercent, value, rawFiveMinute, rawOneHour); }
        private static SignalDecision decisionOf(TradeSignal s) { return s == null || s.getDecision() == null ? SignalDecision.NEUTRAL : s.getDecision(); }
    }
}

1149:      */
1150:     public ExecutionDecision revalidateAtExecutionPrice(TradeSignal signal, ExecutionDecision approvedDecision, BigDecimal executionPrice) {
1151:         // FIX-122: recheck only stamped initial approvals; ADD approvals carry no stamp.
1152:         if (stopLossEvidence != null) approvedDecision = stopLossEvidence.recheck(signal, approvedDecision);
1153:         if (approvedDecision != null && !approvedDecision.allowed()) return approvedDecision;
1154:         if (signal == null || approvedDecision == null || !approvedDecision.allowed()
1155:                 || executionPrice == null || executionPrice.signum() <= 0) {
1156:             return ExecutionDecision.reject("EXECUTION_PRICE_INVALID",
1157:                     "A fresh positive execution price is required before wallet execution.");
1158:         }

1161:                     "Current execution price is already at/below the signal stop; BUY cancelled.", approvedDecision.evidence());
1162:         }

1164:             return ExecutionDecision.reject("EXECUTION_PRICE_AT_OR_ABOVE_TARGET",
1165:                     "Current execution price has already reached/exceeded the signal target; BUY cancelled.", approvedDecision.evidence());
1166:         }
1167:         EntryQuality quality = assessEntryQualityAtPrice(signal, executionPrice);
1168:         return applyInitialEntryQualityGuard(approvedDecision, quality).withStopBoundary(approvedDecision.stopBoundary());
1169:     }

2155:     private ImmediatePriceAction immediatePriceAction(TradeSignal signal) {
2156:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null || signal.getAtrAtSignal() == null
2157:                 || signal.getAtrAtSignal().signum() <= 0) {

2159:         }

2162:                 signal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, signal.getGeneratedAt(),
2163:                 PageRequest.of(0, IMMEDIATE_PRICE_ACTION_LOOKBACK_CANDLES));
2164:         if (candles == null || candles.size() < 2) {
2165:             return ImmediatePriceAction.supportive("insufficient closed-candle history; existing behavior preserved");
2166:         }

2169:         Candle previous = candles.get(1);
2170:         if (latest.getOpenPrice() == null || latest.getHighPrice() == null || latest.getLowPrice() == null
2171:                 || latest.getClosePrice() == null || previous.getClosePrice() == null) {
2172:             return ImmediatePriceAction.supportive("incomplete closed-candle OHLC; existing behavior preserved");

2176:         BigDecimal range = latest.getHighPrice().subtract(latest.getLowPrice()).abs();

2178:                 : latest.getClosePrice().subtract(latest.getLowPrice())
2179:                         .divide(range, 12, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).doubleValue();
2180:         closeLocation = Math.max(0d, Math.min(1d, closeLocation));
2181: 
2182:         double bearishBodyAtr = latest.getOpenPrice().subtract(latest.getClosePrice())
2183:                 .max(BigDecimal.ZERO).divide(atr, 12, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).doubleValue();

2188:                 .orElse(latest.getHighPrice());

2190:                 .max(BigDecimal.ZERO).divide(atr, 12, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).doubleValue();
2191: 
2192:         boolean closeBelowPrevious = latest.getClosePrice().compareTo(previous.getClosePrice()) < 0;
2193:         boolean bearishRejection = latest.getClosePrice().compareTo(latest.getOpenPrice()) < 0
2194:                 && closeBelowPrevious
2195:                 && bearishBodyAtr >= IMMEDIATE_PRICE_ACTION_MIN_BEARISH_BODY_ATR
2196:                 && closeLocation <= IMMEDIATE_PRICE_ACTION_MAX_CLOSE_LOCATION_FOR_BEARISH_REJECTION;
2197:         boolean exhaustedPop = closeBelowPrevious
2198:                 && recentHighFadeAtr >= IMMEDIATE_PRICE_ACTION_MIN_RECENT_HIGH_FADE_ATR
2199:                 && closeLocation <= IMMEDIATE_PRICE_ACTION_MAX_CLOSE_LOCATION_FOR_EXHAUSTED_POP;
2200:

2202:                 + ", bearishBody=" + format(bearishBodyAtr) + " ATR"
2203:                 + ", fadeFromRecentHigh=" + format(recentHighFadeAtr) + " ATR"
2204:                 + ", closeVsPrevious=" + (closeBelowPrevious ? "LOWER" : "NOT_LOWER");

2208:                     + (bearishRejection ? ", bearish rejection" : "")
2209:                     + (exhaustedPop ? ", exhausted-pop fade" : ""));

2241:     private Evidence evidence(TradeSignal current) {
2242:         // FIX-122: initial-entry scope only; additions keep their original evidence.
2243:         if (stopLossEvidence != null) stopLossEvidence.requireCurrent(current);
2244:         Instant cutoff = current.getGeneratedAt().minus(EVIDENCE_WINDOW);
2245:         List<TradeSignal> recent = recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt());
2246: 
2247:         int buy = 0, watch = 0, neutral = 0, bearish = 0, evidenceScore = 0;
2248:         int scoreTotal = 0, confidenceTotal = 0, qualityCount = 0;
2249:         int health = OPPORTUNITY_HEALTH_START;
2250:         Instant lastBearishAt = null;
2251:         Instant oldestObservedAt = null;
2252:         List<Long> signalIds = new ArrayList<>();
2253:         List<TradeSignal> observedSequence = new ArrayList<>();
2254: 
2255:         for (TradeSignal signal : recent) {
2256:             if (signal.getGeneratedAt() == null || signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(current.getGeneratedAt())) continue;
2257:             if (signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(cutoff)) break;
2258:             if (stopLossEvidence != null && !stopLossEvidence.eligible(signal, "EVIDENCE")) continue;
2259: 
2260:             if (oldestObservedAt == null || signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(oldestObservedAt)) {
2261:                 oldestObservedAt = signal.getGeneratedAt();
2262:             }
2263: 
2264:             observedSequence.add(signal);
2265: 
2266:             SignalDecision decision = signal.getDecision();
2267:             SignalDecision original = signal.getOriginalDecision();
2268:             SignalDecision effective = strongerDecision(decision, original);
2269: 
2270:             if (effective == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) {
2271:                 bearish++;
2272:                 health += scaledHealthContribution(HEALTH_1M_STRONG_SELL, signal);
2273:                 if (lastBearishAt == null) lastBearishAt = signal.getGeneratedAt();
2274:                 break; // a strong bearish 1m event is still a true memory boundary
2275:             }
2276: 
2277:             if (effective == SignalDecision.SELL) {
2278:                 bearish++;
2279:                 evidenceScore -= WEAK_SELL_EVIDENCE_PENALTY;
2280:                 health += scaledHealthContribution(HEALTH_1M_SELL, signal);
2281:                 if (lastBearishAt == null) lastBearishAt = signal.getGeneratedAt();
2282:                 continue;
2283:             }
2284: 
2285:             if (isBullish(effective)) {
2286:                 buy++;
2287:                 evidenceScore += 3;
2288:                 health += scaledHealthContribution(HEALTH_1M_BUY, signal);
2289:                 if (signal.getId() != null) {
2290:                     signalIds.add(signal.getId());
2291:                 }
2292:             } else if (decision == SignalDecision.WATCH
2293:                     && signal.getTotalScore() >= WATCH_EVIDENCE_MIN_SCORE
2294:                     && signal.getConfidenceScore() >= WATCH_EVIDENCE_MIN_CONFIDENCE) {
2295:                 watch++;
2296:                 evidenceScore += 1;
2297:                 health += scaledHealthContribution(HEALTH_1M_WATCH, signal);
2298:                 if (signal.getId() != null) {
2299:                     signalIds.add(signal.getId());
2300:                 }
2301:             } else if (decision == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL) {
2302:                 neutral++;
2303:                 health += HEALTH_1M_NEUTRAL;
2304:             }
2305: 
2306:             if (decision == SignalDecision.WATCH || isBullish(decision)) {
2307:                 scoreTotal += signal.getTotalScore();
2308:                 confidenceTotal += signal.getConfidenceScore();
2309:                 qualityCount++;
2310:             }
2311:         }
2312: 
2313:         int evidenceMomentum = calculateEvidenceMomentum(observedSequence);
2314:         health += evidenceMomentum;
2315: 
2316:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(current, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE);
2317:         TradeSignal one = latestAtOrBefore(current, TREND_INTERVAL, ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE);
2318: 
2319:         // Higher timeframe evidence actively restores or weakens opportunity health.
2320:         // This is deliberately generic: no symbol-specific values are used.
2321:         health += timeframeHealthContribution(five, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
2322:         health += timeframeHealthContribution(one, TREND_INTERVAL);
2323: 
2324:         long ageMinutes = oldestObservedAt == null ? 0
2325:                 : Math.max(0, Duration.between(oldestObservedAt, current.getGeneratedAt()).toMinutes());
2326:         // Slow decay: fresh bullish evidence should be able to recover faster than age destroys it.
2327:         health -= Math.min(6, (int) (ageMinutes / 10));
2328:         evidenceScore = Math.max(0, evidenceScore);
2329:         health = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, health));
2330: 
2331:         int previousHealth = currentOpportunity(current.getSymbol(), List.of("BUILDING", "WEAKENING", "BLOCKED", "CONFIRMED"))
2332:                 // FIX-122: do not compare fresh health to a pre-stop opportunity snapshot.
2333:                 .filter(o -> stopLossEvidence == null || stopLossEvidence.eligible(o.getLatestSignal(), "PREVIOUS_HEALTH"))
2334:                 .map(ExecutionOpportunity::getOpportunityHealth)
2335:                 .orElse(OPPORTUNITY_HEALTH_START);
2336:         int healthMomentum = health - previousHealth;
2337: 
2338:         return new Evidence(
2339:                 buy + watch + neutral + bearish,
2340:                 buy,
2341:                 watch,
2342:                 neutral,
2343:                 bearish,
2344:                 evidenceScore,
2345:                 health,
2346:                 healthMomentum,
2347:                 evidenceMomentum,
2348:                 qualityCount == 0 ? 0 : Math.round((float) scoreTotal / qualityCount),
2349:                 qualityCount == 0 ? 0 : Math.round((float) confidenceTotal / qualityCount),
2350:                 five == null ? null : five.getDecision(),
2351:                 one == null ? null : one.getDecision(),
2352:                 lastBearishAt,
2353:                 signalIds.stream().filter(Objects::nonNull).toList()
2354:         );
2355:     }
